By expanding the state space, the model captures HARQ round-trip time and other cross-slot timing effects. The framework determines the transmission opportunities required to satisfy heterogeneous reliability and latency constraints and supports offset-based scheduling through a two-stage genetic algorithm.
  Simulations with industrial URLLC traffic show that the proposed method achieves higher schedulability than reactive HARQ, K-Repetition, and non-guaranteed proactive HARQ, with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [173] [Closed-Loop Decision-Focused Learning for User-Aware Cloud Orchestration under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05735)
*Dongbin Jiao,Xubo Zhang,Huakang Lin,Ke Shang,Shi Yan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出CL-DFL框架，通过将MTGNN时空预测器与基于TPE的零阶决策聚焦学习相结合，建立端到端的闭环反馈通路，并引入GRPO增强的GNeuro-PLS局部搜索策略，解决云环境中异构作业调度的多目标组合优化问题，在四个真实数据集上取得了优于现有方法的违规率、用户满意度和资源利用率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云工作负载随时间变化，导致淡季资源利用不足和旺季资源争用。现有的预测-优化（PTO）框架存在两阶段解耦问题，难以在违规率、用户满意度和资源利用率之间取得平衡。

Method: 1) 将异构作业调度建模为不确定约束下的多目标组合优化问题（MOCOP）；2) 提出CL-DFL闭环决策聚焦学习框架，集成MTGNN时空预测器和基于树状Parzen估计器（TPE）的零阶决策聚焦学习机制，建立资源感知与调度决策间的端到端反馈通路；3) 开发GNeuro-PLS策略，将组相对策略优化（GRPO）融入协作式局部搜索以提升异构工作负载下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，CL-DFL在违规率、用户满意度和资源利用率之间取得了优越的权衡，在常规工作负载下有效控制过载风险，在高饱和场景下保持鲁棒性，优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: CL-DFL通过端到端的闭环反馈机制解决了PTO框架的两阶段解耦问题，在云编排场景中实现了资源感知与调度决策的有效协同，为不确定环境下的多目标优化调度提供了新范式。

Abstract: Time-varying cloud workloads often cause resource under-utilization during off-peak periods and resource contention during peak periods. Existing prediction-then-optimization (PTO) frameworks suffer from two-stage decoupling, hindering the balance among violation rate, user satisfaction, and resource utilization. We formulate heterogeneous job scheduling as a multi-objective combinatorial optimization problem (MOCOP) under uncertain constraints and propose a closed-loop decision-focused learning (CL-DFL) framework for cloud orchestration. CL-DFL integrates a Multivariate Time-series Graph Neural Network (MTGNN)-based spatio-temporal predictor with a zeroth-order decision-focused learning (DFL) mechanism based on the tree-structured Parzen estimator (TPE). This integration establishes an end-to-end (E2E) feedback pathway between resource perception and scheduling decisions. Furthermore, we develop the GNeuro-PLS strategy by incorporating group relative policy optimization (GRPO) into cooperative local search to improve robustness under heterogeneous workloads. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that CL-DFL achieves superior trade-offs among violation rate, user satisfaction, and resource utilization. It effectively controls overload risks under regular workloads and maintains resilience under highly saturated scenarios compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [174] [5G ISAC-Based UAV Detection and 3-D Tracking Using Uplink Sounding Reference Signals on an End-to-End O-RAN Simulation Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05826)
*Arun K. Gurung,Satha K. Sathananthan,Shiva R. Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端的O-RAN仿真测试平台，用于5G集成感知与通信（ISAC），针对低空无人机检测和3D跟踪。该平台基于开源组件（OpenAirInterface、FlexRIC和Sionna RT）构建，将NR上行探测参考信号（SRS）重新用作无源雷达波形，无需改变NR标准或增加专用感知波形。通过两种独立方法（平面接收阵列和第二发射机）解决单双基地对下仰角不可观测的问题，实验证明高度可观测性在信号处理链和实时栈中均得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC允许蜂窝基础设施在同一波形上同时服务于通信用户和感知功能，但现有工作中缺乏一个端到端的、基于开放标准的测试平台来验证ISAC在实际5G系统中的可行性，特别是在低空无人机检测和3D跟踪场景下。

Method: 利用OpenAirInterface、FlexRIC和Sionna RT构建端到端O-RAN仿真测试平台。将NR上行SRS重新用作无源雷达波形，在gNB内部署PHY层感知阶段产生检测结果，通过自定义E2服务模型将检测送到执行扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）的跟踪xApp。为解决单双基地对下仰角不可观测问题，采用两种方案：平面接收阵列提供垂直孔径，以及第二发射机提供距离分集。

Result: 两种方案的实际结果均与离线射线追踪研究一致，估计器从故意错误的初始高度收敛到1.8米RMSE，滤波器表现一致，证明高度可观测性在信号处理链和实时端到端栈中均得到验证。在并发的10 Mbps上行通信负载下，检测覆盖得以保持。

Conclusion: 本文验证了在不修改NR标准、不增加专用感知波形的前提下，利用现有5G基础设施实现低空无人机ISAC 3D跟踪的可行性，为O-RAN架构下的ISAC部署提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) lets cellular infrastructure serve communication users and sense on the same waveform. We present an end-to-end O-RAN simulation testbed for 5G ISAC targeting low-altitude UAV detection and 3-D tracking, built from open-source components: OpenAirInterface, FlexRIC and Sionna RT, in which the NR Uplink Sounding Reference Signal is repurposed as a passive radar waveform: a PHY-layer sensing stage inside the gNB produces detections that reach an Extended Kalman Filter tracking xApp over a custom E2 service model, with no change to the NR standard and no dedicated sensing waveform. A single bistatic pair leaves elevation unobservable, so the tracker needs a height prior; we remove it two independent ways and measure both - a planar receive array supplying a vertical aperture, and a second transmitter supplying range diversity. Both live results corroborate an offline ray-traced study of the same estimator, which converges from a deliberately wrong initial altitude to 1.8 m RMSE at a consistent filter, so altitude observability is established both in the signal-processing chain and end to end through the live stack. Detection coverage is preserved under a concurrent 10 Mbps uplink communications load.

</details>


### [175] [BALANCE: Hybrid Autoregressive-Speculative LLM Inference in Wireless Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05926)
*Guanqiao Qu,Shuo Chen,Qian Chen,Kin K. Leung,Xianhao Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出BALANCE框架，在边缘服务器上同时部署SLM和LLM，通过混合自回归解码（AD）和投机解码（SD）两种推理模式，在延迟-内存权衡下最大化服务用户数量。通过将NP-hard的吞吐量最大化问题分解为两个子问题，提出多项式时间算法获得带常数近似比的次优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘推理中，自回归解码（AD）延迟长但内存开销低，投机解码（SD）速度快但需要额外内存。在有限的边缘计算资源下，面对异构用户需求，单一推理模式无法高效服务所有用户，存在延迟与内存之间的两难权衡。

Method: 提出BALANCE混合推理框架：边缘服务器同时托管SLM和LLM，将用户分配到AD或SD模式并并行执行；将其建模为任务吞吐量最大化问题，联合优化用户调度和AD/SD间的计算资源分配，满足用户延迟要求和服务器内存约束；由于问题NP-hard，将原问题分解为两个子问题，设计多项式时间算法获得带常数近似比的次优解。

Result: 实验表明BALANCE一致优于传统的纯AD和纯SD方法，显著提升了任务吞吐量。

Conclusion: 通过混合AD与SD两种推理模式并联合优化调度与资源分配，BALANCE在边缘LLM推理场景中有效缓解了延迟-内存权衡，为异构用户需求下的高效服务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Edge inference is a promising paradigm to provide large language model (LLM) inference services in next-generation mobile networks. LLM inference mainly relies on two approaches: Autoregressive decoding (AD) generates output tokens sequentially, resulting in long latency; Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates inference by using a small language model (SLM) to generate multiple draft tokens for LLM verification, but incurs extra memory costs. Due to this latency-memory tradeoff, neither approach alone can efficiently serve users with heterogeneous demands under limited edge computing resources. To address this challenge, we propose a hybrid autoregressive-speculative inference (BALANCE) framework for edge LLM inference. In BALANCE, an edge server hosts both an SLM and an LLM, assigns each user to AD or SD, and performs the two modes simultaneously. To maximize the number of served users, we formulate a task throughput maximization problem to jointly determine user scheduling and computing resource allocation between AD and SD under user latency requirements and server memory constraints. Since the problem is NP-hard, we develop a polynomial-time algorithm that transforms the original problem into two sub-problems and obtains a sub-optimal solution with a constant approximation guarantee. Experiments demonstrate that BALANCE consistently outperforms conventional AD and SD and significantly improves task throughput.

</details>


### [176] [ML-for-ML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06046)
*Yutong Zhao,Noga H. Rotman,Gianni Antichi,Ran Ben Basat*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出ML-for-ML，一种跨层联合优化视角，将网络侧和ML侧的参数在共享的"到达目标损失时间"目标下联合选择。初步原型显示，通过联合优化ML和网络参数，可最多快42%达到目标损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI训练负载快速增长，时间和能源成本日益重要。在共享云集群中，训练任务与共运行负载竞争网络资源，但网络机制和ML训练选择通常被分别优化：网络控制字节如何移动，ML系统控制何时及通信多少。这种分离导致端到端性能未被充分利用。

Method: 提出ML-for-ML跨层视角，将网络侧和ML侧的旋钮（knobs）在共享的"时间到目标损失"（time-to-target-loss）目标下联合选择，实现网络与ML参数的协同优化。

Result: 初步原型表明，通过联合优化ML和网络参数，达到目标损失的速度最多可提升42%。

Conclusion: 网络和ML系统的分离优化会损失端到端性能，跨层联合优化是一种有前景的方向，值得进一步探索。

Abstract: AI training workloads are growing rapidly, making their time, energy, and infrastructure costs increasingly important. In shared cloud clusters, training and fine-tuning jobs compete with co-running workloads for network resources, while network mechanisms and ML training choices are typically optimized separately: networking controls how bytes move, whereas ML systems control when and how much communication occurs. We argue that this separation leaves end-to-end performance on the table.
  We present ML-for-ML, a cross-layer perspective in which network-side and ML-side knobs are selected jointly under a shared time-to-target-loss objective. Our preliminary prototype shows that by co-optimizing the ML and network parameters, we reach the target loss up to 42% faster.

</details>


### [177] [MARS: Multipath Adaptive Reliable Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06101)
*Yitong Li,Xinjiao Li,Dirk Kutscher*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MARS是一种接收端驱动的、转发器辅助的多路径传输协议，用于互联网/WAN环境。它结合了分层同步的覆盖路径发现与耦合的消费者/转发器拥塞控制，以增量部署的UDP覆盖形式运行。实验表明，在仅端点部署时与端到端多路径基线相当；在协作转发器下，可将最大p95流完成时间降低高达81.5%，并展现出更强的鲁棒性和快速故障恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有互联网/WAN大数据传输面临权衡：端到端传输（如MPTCP、MPQUIC）可部署但受限于端点可见路径和延迟的拥塞反馈；而路由/转发辅助方法需要基础设施支持或缺乏安全的转发协调。

Method: MARS采用接收端驱动、转发器辅助的设计，包含两个核心组件：1）分层同步的覆盖路径发现机制；2）耦合的消费者/转发器拥塞控制。它以UDP覆盖形式增量部署在客户端、服务器、中继或CDN节点上。

Result: 大规模仿真和Mininet仿真实验表明：端点部署时与端到端多路径基线竞争力相当；有协作转发器时，相较于ECMP受限基线，最大p95流完成时间降低高达81.5%；与拥有相同路径集的路径扩展端到端基线相比，MARS实现更低的极端p95 FCT，在丢包下具有更强鲁棒性，并能快速从瞬时链路故障中恢复。

Conclusion: ICN风格的接收端驱动转发可以作为WAN多路径的可部署覆盖传输基础，在无需修改IP路由的情况下提供超越纯端到端设计的优势。

Abstract: Multipath transport is increasingly important for Internet/WAN services that move large data volumes across heterogeneous paths, including geo-distributed analytics, content distribution, and cloud-service pipelines. Existing solutions, however, face a practical trade-off: end-to-end transports such as MPTCP and MPQUIC are deployable but limited by endpoint-visible paths and delayed congestion feedback, while routing-or forwarder-assisted approaches often require infrastructure support or lack safe coordination across forwarding choices. This paper presents MARS, a receiver-driven, forwarder-assisted multipath transport for Internet/WAN environments. MARS combines tier-synchronized overlay path discovery with coupled consumer/forwarder congestion control, enabling it to safely expand usable forwarding opportunities and react near bottlenecks. It runs as an incrementally deployable UDP overlay at clients, servers, relays, or CDN-like nodes. We implement MARS in simulation and as a prototype, and evaluate it through large-scale simulation and Mininet emulation under different deployment scales, loss rates, and failure scenarios. The results show that MARS provides deployment-dependent benefits: with endpoint-only deployment, it remains competitive with end-to-end multipath baselines; with cooperating overlay forwarders, it exposes richer usable path diversity and reduces max p95 flow completion time by up to 81.5\% over ECMP-limited baselines. Even against path-expanded end-to-end baselines given the same path set, MARS achieves lower worst-case p95 FCT and stronger robustness under packet loss, while also recovering quickly from transient link failures. These results demonstrate that ICN-style receiver-driven forwarding can serve as a deployable overlay transport substrate for WAN multipath, providing benefits beyond purely end-to-end designs without requiring changes to IP routing.

</details>


### [178] [MultiMoQ: Multi-Access Media-Over-QUIC for Robust Immersive Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06102)
*Yitong Li,Xinjiao Li,Ruonan Chai,Dirk Kutscher*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MultiMoQ是一个基于MoQ的多接入平铺流媒体框架，通过重新设计传输机制以支持高码率沉浸式流媒体的可靠传输，实现灵活的平铺调度和无缝接入切换，相比DASH和标准MoQ显著提升了吞吐量、降低了延迟并避免了播放停滞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的360度视频流媒体方案难以支持大量并发用户的高码率沉浸式流媒体需求，存在交付粒度粗糙、响应性不足以及并发平铺流协调支持不够的问题。虽然MoQ在大规模媒体交付中展现出潜力，但在带宽受限条件下缺乏鲁棒性，常常导致播放停滞。

Method: MultiMoQ重新设计了基于MoQ的交付机制，利用多接入路径实现鲁棒的高码率流媒体传输，同时支持灵活的平铺调度和无缝的接入切换。论文实现了一个完整功能原型，并在异构真实网络条件下的网络仿真中进行了评估，与DASH和标准MoQ进行了对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，相比DASH，MultiMoQ提高了增强层平铺和基础视频的吞吐量，降低了增强层平铺的尾部端到端延迟，同时保持音频连续性，避免了标准MoQ的持续停滞问题。这些传输改进转化为更流畅的视口播放，消融实验进一步证实了多接入控制对播放连续性的贡献。

Conclusion: MultiMoQ通过多接入路径设计和灵活调度机制有效解决了高码率沉浸式流媒体在带宽受限条件下的可靠性问题，为大规模沉浸式视频流媒体提供了一种更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Live immersive video streaming, particularly 360-degree video, is increasingly adopted in applications such as virtual events, sports broadcasting, and remote education. Existing approaches struggle to support high-bitrate immersive streaming for large numbers of concurrent users, with coarse-grained delivery limiting responsiveness and insufficient support for coordinating concurrent tile streams. Media over QUIC (MoQ) has recently emerged as a promising solution for large-scale media delivery, yet it lacks robustness under bandwidth-constrained conditions, often resulting in playback stalls. To address these challenges, we present MultiMoQ, a multi-access tile streaming framework built on MoQ that redesigns its delivery mechanism for robust high-bitrate streaming across multiple access paths while supporting flexible tile scheduling and seamless access switching. We implement a fully functional prototype of MultiMoQ and evaluate it in network emulation under heterogeneous real-world network conditions, comparing against Dynamic Adaptive Streaming over HTTP (DASH) and standard MoQ. Results show that MultiMoQ increases goodput for enhancement tiles and base video and reduces enhancement-tile tail end-to-end latency relative to DASH, while preserving audio continuity and avoiding the persistent stalls of standard MoQ. These transport gains translate into smoother viewport playback, and the ablation results further confirm the contribution of multi-access control to playback continuity.

</details>


### [179] [From Passive Mirrors to Active Agents: Holonic Digital Twins for Physical AI over Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06227)
*Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Omar Hashash,Walid Saad*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HDT-Nets（全息数字孪生网络）的框架，旨在解决AI在物理系统中（如机器人、车辆）因缺乏可靠世界模型而失败的问题。该框架通过分层结构的全息智能体实现实时物理AI推理，利用因果马尔可夫毯、主动推理、范畴论和整合信息论等理论，使智能体既能自主推理又能协作形成集体智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI工具（包括深度学习和生成式AI）在嵌入物理系统时仍会失败，因为它们无法在不确定条件下维持长期规划所需的世界模型，也无法泛化到未见场景。当前的无线网络架构仅优化吞吐量、延迟和可靠性，无法支持需要智能体维护共享时空上下文的实时物理AI协调。

Method: 提出HDT-Nets框架，核心方法包括：1）构建覆盖物理智能体和网络边缘的分层全息数字孪生结构，每个HDT在本地自主推理并与邻近HDT协作；2）利用覆盖感知、通信和控制的因果马尔可夫毯决定哪些智能体需要协调，并支持跨多领域干预的反事实推理；3）通过最小化预期自由能的主动推理统一感知、行动和学习，并依据认知价值决定传输哪些信念；4）运用范畴论确保异构智能体间传输的信念保持语义结构；5）通过整合信息论量化集体智能何时超越独立运作。

Result: 论文提出的是一个概念性框架，尚未给出具体实验数据，但论述了如何将无线网络从传统的通信基础设施转变为支持物理智能协调的"神经系统"，使网络能够编排物理世界的实时AI推理。

Conclusion: HDT-Nets为无线网络赋能物理智能提供了系统性的理论框架，通过多层理论（主动推理、范畴论、整合信息论）的结合，为未来物理AI系统的实时协调与集体智能涌现奠定了基础。

Abstract: Despite advances in artificial intelligence (AI) across multiple sectors, today's AI tools, including deep learning and generative AI, still fail when embedded into physical systems, such as robots and vehicles operating under real-world physical laws. This stems from their inability to maintain reliable world models for long-horizon planning under uncertainty and generalize to unseen scenarios. In this context, wireless networks, through pervasive sensing and communication, can orchestrate physical intelligence. However, current architectures optimize throughput, latency, and reliability and cannot support real-time physical AI coordination, requiring agents to maintain shared spatiotemporal context. To address these challenges, a network of holonic digital twins (HDT-Nets) framework is proposed to deliver real-time physical AI inference through holonic agents that actively reason about their environment rather than passively mirror physical assets. Each HDT is realized as a hierarchical structure spanning the physical agent and network edge, reasoning autonomously at the local level while cooperating with neighboring HDTs to form collectively intelligent units. In HDT-Net, causal Markov blankets spanning sensing, communication, and control determine which agents must coordinate and enable counterfactual reasoning over multi-domain interventions. Active inference within these boundaries unifies perception, action, and learning by minimizing expected free energy while deciding which beliefs to transmit based on their cognitive value to the receiver. Category theory ensures that transmitted beliefs preserve semantic structure across heterogeneous agents with incompatible representations. Finally, integrated information theory quantifies when collective intelligence exceeds independent operation and how network intelligence evolves through coordinated learning and information exchange.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Likelihood-based anomaly detection in preferential attachment networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05182)
*Qiu Liang,Remco van der Hofstad,Nelly Litvak*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究优先连接（PA）网络中的异常顶点检测问题。异常顶点在特定时间点出现，并因参数β获得额外的连接优势，而普通顶点遵循参数δ的PA机制。作者提出了迭代参数估计方法结合基于似然的检测框架，模拟结果显示该方法能准确估计参数β和δ，检测性能取决于异常发生时间：网络演化中期出现的异常最易被检测，而早期和晚期异常更具挑战性，尤其当β较小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优先连接网络广泛用于建模真实网络增长动态，但现有研究较少关注网络中异常顶点的检测问题。由于PA网络中顶点度增长具有高变异性，且当异常在演化后期出现时可用信息有限，检测此类异常具有挑战性。

Method: 提出迭代参数估计过程，结合基于似然的检测框架。异常顶点在出现时获得由参数β控制的额外连接优势，普通顶点遵循参数δ的PA机制。通过似然函数来区分异常与正常顶点。

Result: 模拟结果表明：（1）所提出的程序能准确估计参数β和δ；（2）检测性能依赖于异常发生时间：中期出现的异常检测最可靠；（3）极早期和极晚期的异常检测仍具挑战性，尤其是当β较小时。

Conclusion: 该研究为PA网络中的异常顶点检测提供了可行方案，但检测性能受异常出现时间和额外优势参数β大小的影响，未来可能需要针对早期和晚期异常开发更鲁棒的检测方法。

Abstract: Preferential attachment (PA) network is a widely used model for capturing the growth dynamics of real-world networks, in which newly arriving vertices are more likely to connect to existing vertices with higher degrees. In this paper, we consider a setting in which an anomalous vertex appears at some time point and receives edges with an additional attachment advantage governed by a parameter $β$, while ordinary vertices continue to follow the PA mechanism with parameter $δ$. Detecting such anomalies is challenging due to the high variability in degree growth in PA networks and the limited information available when the anomaly arrives late in the network evolution. We propose an iterative parameter estimation procedure together with a likelihood-based detection framework. Simulation results show that the proposed procedure provides accurate estimation of the parameters $β$ and $δ$. Detection performance depends on the time of anomaly occurrence: anomalies arising at midway stages of the network evolution are detected most reliably, whereas very early and late anomalies remain challenging, particularly when the parameter $β$ is small.

</details>


### [181] [Automatic Detection of Deaths from Social Networking Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05183)
*Nuhu Ibrahim,Riza Batista-Navarro*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了社交媒体用户在生前和死后发布内容之间的语言特征差异，并开发了自动检测社交网络用户死亡的机器学习分类器，其中BERT模型表现最佳，取得了极高的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏对生前与死后社交媒体内容语言特征差异的系统性分析，且自动检测社交网络用户死亡的任务具有重要的社会和应用价值（如数字遗产管理、用户关怀等）。

Method: 使用Wikidata和Twitter构建新数据集；采用传统ML模型（RF、KNN、LR、SVM）和深度学习模型（BiLSTM、CNN、BERT）；使用TF-IDF和预训练词嵌入（Glove、Word2Vec、FastText）提取特征，对生前与死后内容进行分类，并进行语言学特征分析（情感、词类、心理语言特征等）。

Result: RF在传统模型中表现最佳；BiLSTM优于CNN；传统模型中TF-IDF优于预训练嵌入，深度学习中Word2Vec优于Glove和FastText；BERT超越所有其他模型。死后推文负面情感更高、负面情绪词更多；死后内容更频繁使用人称代词、动词、家庭/宗教/死亡/咒骂词，生前内容更多使用非人称代词和口语词；死后对话更体现分析性思维。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了高性能的社交网络用户死亡自动检测技术，为数字遗产管理、用户关怀等应用提供了重要工具，并对生前/死后语言特征差异提供了深入洞察。

Abstract: This dissertation analysed and discussed the differences in linguistic characteristics between pre-mortem and post-mortem social media content, and reported machine learning (ML) classifiers that achieved high performance in automatically detecting deaths of social networking site users from posts associated with their profiles. A new dataset was developed using Wikidata and Twitter. ML models, both traditional (RF, KNN, LR, and SVM) and deep learning (BiLSTM, CNN, and the state-of-the-art BERT), were trained on features extracted using TF-IDF and pre-trained embeddings (Glove, Word2Vec, and FastText) to classify post-mortem content from its pre-mortem counterpart. The results showed that RF outperformed all other traditional ML models; BiLSTM outperformed CNN; TF-IDF consistently outperformed pre-trained word embeddings for the traditional models; Word2Vec consistently outperformed Glove and FastText for the deep learning models; and BERT outperformed all other models. It was found that although pre-mortem and post-mortem tweets express similar levels of positive sentiment, post-mortem tweets exhibit higher negative sentiment, whereas pre-mortem tweets exhibit higher neutral sentiment. Feelings suggesting negativity (sad, angry, surprise, and fear) are more dominant in post-mortem tweets, while happy is more dominant in pre-mortem tweets. It was also found that words, personal pronouns, verbs, family words, religious words, death words, and swear words occur more frequently in post-mortem tweets, whereas impersonal pronouns and informal words occur more frequently in pre-mortem tweets. Additionally, analytical thinking is expressed more in post-mortem than pre-mortem conversations. This experiment's significant contribution is the successful development of an exceptionally high-performing technique for automatically detecting user deaths on social networking sites.

</details>


### [182] [IMMENSE: Inductive Multi-perspective User Classification in Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05259)
*Francesco Benedetti,Antonio Pellicani,Gianvito Pio,Michelangelo Ceci*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IMMENSE，一种基于机器学习的混合分类方法，用于检测社交网络中的恶意用户。该方法整合了用户发布内容的语义、社交关系和空间信息三个视角，并采用归纳学习方式，无需重新训练即可分类新用户或新网络。在真实Twitter/X数据集上的实验表明，IMMENSE优于五种最先进的对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社交网络越来越多地使用户接触到传播歧视、仇恨和暴力内容的用户，尤其是年轻用户容易受到伤害。由于社交网络规模庞大，执法机构（LEAs）迫切需要有效的系统来识别和应对传播恶意内容的用户。

Method: IMMENSE采用混合分类策略，整合三个视角：1）用户发布内容的语义信息；2）用户的社交关系；3）用户的空间信息。该方法采用归纳学习方式，使其能够对未见过的用户或全新网络进行分类，无需昂贵的模型重新训练。

Result: 在真实Twitter/X数据集上的实验表明，IMMENSE在检测恶意用户方面优于五种最先进的对比方法，验证了其混合方法在社交网络监控系统中有效部署的优势。

Conclusion: IMMENSE的混合分类方法结合内容语义、社交关系和空间信息有效提升了恶意用户检测性能，且归纳学习特性使其具备良好的扩展性和实际部署价值，可辅助执法机构进行社交网络监控。

Abstract: Online social networks increasingly expose people to users who propagate discriminatory, hateful, and violent content. Young users, in particular, are vulnerable to exposure to such content, which can have harmful psychological and social repercussions. Given the massive scale of today's social networks, in terms of both published content and number of users, there is an urgent need for effective systems to aid Law Enforcement Agencies (LEAs) in identifying and addressing users that disseminate malicious content. In this work we introduce IMMENSE, a machine learning-based method for detecting malicious social network users. Our approach adopts a hybrid classification strategy that integrates three perspectives: the semantics of the users' published content, their social relationships and their spatial information. Such contextual perspectives potentially enhance classification performance beyond text-only analysis. Importantly, IMMENSE employs an inductive learning approach, enabling it to classify previously unseen users or entire new networks without the need for costly and time-consuming model retraining procedures. Experiments carried out on a real-world Twitter/X dataset showed the superiority of IMMENSE against five state of the art competitors, confirming the benefits of its hybrid approach for effective deployment in social network monitoring systems.

</details>


### [183] [SnapScope: A Platform for City-Scale Collection and Exploration of Public Snap Map Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05841)
*Mohammed Almukaynizi,Fahad Alhaqbani,Khaled Almarzoug,Sultan Alanbari*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: SnapScope是一个集后端采集管道与Web前端于一体的综合平台，用于以城市规模收集、管理和探索Snapchat Snap Map上的地理位置标记的公共故事数据。该平台在沙特阿拉伯利雅得部署23天，通过1公里网格的2,740个查询点收集了515,364个独特的公共快照，并提供隐私安全的聚合数据集（CC BY 4.0）。平台具有城市无关性，可替换网格坐标和边界多边形后重新部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Snapchat Snap Map是地理标记公共视频和图像故事的短暂流，但该平台没有文档化的API，此前没有工作描述可复现的城市规模数据收集系统，也没有用于交互式管理和探索收集数据的工具。因此需要一个完整的解决方案来填补这些空白。

Method: 构建集成平台SnapScope：后端采集管道负责数据收集，Web前端提供爬虫管理、交互式数据探索、相邻社区对比和数据导出功能。平台通过1公里网格的2,740个查询点覆盖利雅得市，运行23天进行数据采集，并进行饱和度探测实验验证重复率。

Result: 在23天内收集了515,364个独特的公共快照；21次连续运行的饱和度探测显示94.8%的返回观测是已存储记录的重复；发布了CC BY 4.0许可的隐私安全聚合数据集。

Conclusion: SnapScope证明可以在城市规模上可复现地收集Snap Map数据，平台是城市无关且可重新部署的，为研究Snapchat上的地理社会感知提供了基础设施，同时通过聚合数据集保护用户隐私。

Abstract: Snapchat's Snap Map is an ephemeral stream of geotagged public video and image stories, but the platform provides no documented API, no prior work describes a reproducible system for collecting this data at city scale, and no tool exists for managing and exploring the collected data interactively. We present SnapScope, an integrated platform that pairs a back-end collection pipeline with a web-based front end for scraper management, interactive data exploration, side-by-side neighborhood comparison, and data export. We deploy the platform over Riyadh, Saudi Arabia, collecting 515,364 unique public snaps across 23 days on a 1 km grid of 2,740 query points. A saturation probe over 21 consecutive runs shows that 94.8% of returned observations are duplicates of already-stored records. We provide a privacy-safe aggregate dataset under CC BY 4.0. The platform is city-agnostic and redeployable by substituting grid coordinates and boundary polygons.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Towards a Risk Assessment of Malicious Skill Files in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05223)
*Rui Yang,Michael Fu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Chetan Arora,Joey Chua*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自主编码智能体的"技能接口"（agent skills interface）存在严重安全漏洞——恶意shell命令可以隐藏在看似无害的自然语言技能文件中。作者通过对抗性技能合成方法，利用6个LLM将471个真实shell命令转化为2,826个良性外观的技能基准，并构建了可复现的评估流水线，对两个企业级智能体（Gemini CLI和Qwen Code）进行了5,629次完整运行的测试，结果显示它们分别被利用率达95.5-96.1%和71.6-74.0%，而显式安全识别率仅1.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码智能体正越来越多地嵌入企业软件工作流并拥有连接系统的委托权限，其核心架构中的"技能接口"（skill interface）——包含指令和脚本的文件夹——虽然让智能体动态加载以专业化行为，但也显著扩大了攻击面，使恶意shell命令能够隐藏在自然语言技能文件中。作者旨在系统性地量化和揭示这一安全风险的严重程度。

Method: 三部分方法：(1) 对抗性技能合成：使用跨4个家族的6个LLM，将471个真实shell命令转换为良性外观的技能，构建了涵盖11个MITRE ATT&CK战术的2,826个技能基准；(2) 可复现评估流水线：结合运行分层（run stratification）、证据锚定（evidence anchoring）、拒绝否决（refusal veto）和确定性声明意图覆盖（deterministic declared-intent override），配合三裁判LLM-as-a-judge面板，并通过盲人人类金标准验证（Cohen's kappa=0.85）；(3) 大规模表征：对两个企业级智能体进行5,629次完整运行测试。

Result: Gemini CLI在95.5-96.1%的运行中被利用，Qwen Code在71.6-74.0%的运行中被利用（从原始多数投票到声明意图修正估计，均落在人类金标准范围内），且几乎不受生成模型影响。显式安全识别率仅为1.99%。评估流水线达到了与人类金标准高度一致性（Cohen's kappa=0.85）。

Conclusion: 企业必须在采用编码智能体之前评估和缓解技能接口风险。当前主流企业级编码智能体对隐藏恶意指令的防护能力严重不足，攻击几乎总是成功，且智能体极少显式识别出安全问题，这一发现对企业AI安全治理具有重要的紧迫性。代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly embedded in enterprise software workflows with delegated authority over connected systems. Central to this architecture is the agent skills interface: folders of instructions and scripts that agents load dynamically to specialize their behavior. This interface also widens the attack surface, letting malicious shell commands hide within natural-language skill files. We make three contributions. First, an adversarial skill-synthesis method using six LLMs across four families to transform 471 real-world shell commands into benign-appearing skills, released as a benchmark of 2,826 skills mapped to 11 MITRE ATT&CK tactics. Second, a reproducible evaluation pipeline coupling run stratification, evidence anchoring, a refusal veto, and a deterministic declared-intent override with a three-judge LLM-as-a-judge panel, validated against a blind human gold standard (Cohen's kappa = 0.85). Third, a large-scale characterization of two enterprise-grade agents across 5,629 completed runs. Gemini CLI is exploited in 95.5-96.1% of runs and Qwen Code in 71.6-74.0% (raw majority vote to declared-intent-corrected estimate, both within the human gold standard), nearly invariant to the generating model. Explicit safety recognition occurs in only 1.99% of runs. Enterprises must assess and mitigate skill-interface risk before adopting coding agents. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/awsm-research/AgentJailbreak

</details>


### [185] [Improving Debugging in Verification-Aware Languages Through Automated Fault Localization: A Case Study in Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05399)
*Álvaro Silva,Isabel Amaral,João Pascoal Faria,Alexandra Mendes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了验证感知语言（如Dafny）中的自动化故障定位，对比了基于状态（state-based）和基于反例（counterexample-based）两种定位范式。通过MutDafny生成突变数据集，使用EXAM评分评估，发现基于反例的方法显著优于基于状态的方法，其中结构化单迹排序带来最大提升，多迹聚合则进一步增强鲁棒性和调试效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证感知语言在验证失败时提供的反馈仅限于具体的错误条件（如违反的后置条件），而非故障根本原因。虽然Dafny的反例功能能提供具体执行轨迹，但每次断言失败通常只暴露单一失败路径，开发者仍需手动检查整个轨迹来定位错误。因此需要自动化故障定位方法来提高调试效率。

Method: 提出了两种故障定位范式：(1) 基于状态定位——复现AutoFix的"快照"方法论，通过推断不变量和谓词识别可疑程序状态；(2) 基于反例定位——包含一系列渐进式技术：从原始反例提取，到结构化单迹排名，再到多迹聚合。评估使用MutDafny从DafnyBench生成突变数据集，以EXAM分数衡量定位有效性。

Result: 基于反例的方法在定位效果上显著优于基于状态的方法。单迹结构化排名相比原始反例输出带来了最大改进，多迹聚合通过提高覆盖率和减少求解器引入的路径偏差，进一步增强了鲁棒性和调试效用。

Conclusion: 验证感知语言中的有效故障定位既取决于使用反例信息，也取决于如何对信息进行结构化和多样化处理。这为自动化调试工具的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Verification-aware languages, like Dafny, integrate formal specifications directly into source code to enable static correctness checks. However, when verification fails, the feedback provided is often limited to the specific condition of the error, such as a violated postcondition, rather than the root cause of the fault. While Dafny's counterexample features provide concrete execution traces, these typically expose a single failing path per assertion failure, leaving the developer to manually look through the entire trace to locate the error.
  This paper investigates automated fault localization for verification-aware languages by comparing two paradigms: state-based and counterexample-based localization. Our state-based localization strategy replicates the ``snapshot'' methodology of AutoFix by inferring invariants and predicates to identify suspicious program states. The counterexample-based strategy consists of a family of techniques that progressively enrich the use of verifier output: from raw counterexample extraction, to structured single-trace ranking, and to multi-trace aggregation.
  To validate these methods, we present an evaluation framework using MutDafny to generate a diverse mutant dataset from DafnyBench and measure localization effectiveness using the EXAM score. Our results show that counterexample-based approaches substantially outperform state-based localization in this setting. Structured ranking over a single trace yields the largest improvement over raw counterexample output, while multi-trace aggregation provides additional gains in robustness and debugging utility by increasing coverage and reducing path bias introduced by the solver. These findings demonstrate that effective fault localization in verification-aware languages depends both on using counterexample information, and how that information is structured and diversified.

</details>


### [186] [Reasoning from Traces: Divergence-Guided Agentic Repair of WebAssembly Discrepancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05521)
*Liyan Huang,Kaicheng Wang,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WasmMend是首个自动修复Native与Wasm运行时功能差异的系统，通过差分追踪分析定位分歧函数，再引导LLM代理进行根因分析和补丁生成，在真实C/C++项目上达到70.0%的修复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly承诺C/C++代码可无缝跨编译为可移植、快速且沙箱化的二进制文件，但实际中交叉编译常导致运行时行为差异，根因在于平台级运行时且隐藏在源代码之下，即使最先进的LLM修复代理也难以修复这些差异。

Method: WasmMend采用两阶段方法：首先通过新颖的差分追踪分析方法定位Wasm与原生执行首次出现分歧的函数；然后由LLM代理基于该定位信息进行根因推理并生成消除分歧行为的补丁，将无方向的探索转化为聚焦的推理任务。

Result: 在真实C/C++项目上的实验表明，WasmMend的修复率达70.0%，优于agentic基线的50.2%以及结合修复时LLM插桩方法的54.5%，验证了分歧引导推理对跨平台修复的价值。

Conclusion: 分歧引导的推理方法能显著提升跨平台（Native-Wasm）功能差异的自动修复效果，证明了将定位与修复解耦且以差分分析为核心的系统设计在跨平台软件修复中的有效性。

Abstract: WebAssembly (Wasm) promises seamless reuse of C/C++ codebases as portable, fast, sandboxed binaries. In practice, however, this promise often falls short: recent studies show that cross-compiling the same C/C++ source to Wasm and native binaries frequently leads to runtime discrepancies, owing to library implementation differences or compiler bugs. Since the root causes lie in the platform-level runtime and are hidden beneath the source code, even state-of-the-art LLM-based repair agents often fail to fix these discrepancies. In this paper, we present WasmMend, the first system to automatically repair Native-Wasm functional discrepancies. WasmMend converts the undirected exploration to a focused reasoning task in two stages: First, a novel differential trace analysis approach localizes the function where Wasm and native executions initially diverge; guided by this localization, LLM agents then reason about the root causes and generate patches that eliminate the divergent behavior. Experiments on real-world C/C++ projects show that WasmMend achieves a fix rate of 70.0%, compared to 50.2% for the agentic baseline and 54.5\% for the approach augmented with repair-time LLM-based instrumentation, demonstrating the value of divergence-guided reasoning for cross-platform repair.

</details>


### [187] [Exploring Dependence, Overreliance, and Addiction Related Behaviors Associated with Large Language Model Use Among Software Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05561)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Matheus de Moraes Leça,Cleyton Magalhaes,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对119名软件从业者的探索性调查，探讨了软件工程师在专业开发中使用大语言模型（LLM）时出现的依赖、过度依赖和成瘾相关行为模式，发现功能性依赖最为普遍，过度依赖次之，而成瘾相关行为较少见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛融入软件开发实践，但其在专业工作流中的引入也引发了关于开发者对工具依赖、过度依赖和成瘾相关行为的担忧，目前缺乏对这一现象的实证研究。

Method: 采用探索性调查方法，对119名软件从业人员进行问卷调查，通过描述性统计和定性主题分析对参与者的开放式回答进行分析。

Result: 参与者主要表现出功能性依赖，LLM因生产力和效率优势已融入日常开发活动；存在与过度依赖一致的行为模式（优先于文档查阅和同行咨询，但仍验证生成输出）；成瘾相关行为报告较少，主要表现为难以节制使用或情感依恋，而非失控。

Conclusion: LLM正成为软件工程专业实践中的习惯性组成部分，大多数使用属功能性依赖，但需要促进适当依赖，通过支持信任校准、专业判断和开发全过程的验证来防范风险。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) has changed how software engineers perform everyday development activities. While these systems provide substantial support for tasks such as code generation, debugging, and documentation, their increasing integration into professional workflows has also raised questions regarding developers' reliance on these tools and the emergence of dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. This study investigates how software engineers experience the use of LLMs during professional software development, with attention to behavioral patterns associated with dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. An exploratory survey was conducted with 119 software practitioners. The data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and qualitative thematic analysis of participants' open-ended responses. Participants primarily described functional dependence, with LLMs becoming integrated into routine software engineering activities because of the productivity and efficiency they provide. Responses also suggested patterns consistent with overreliance, particularly through prioritizing LLMs over documentation or peer consultation while continuing to verify generated outputs. Reports associated with addiction-related behaviors were less common and primarily reflected difficulty moderating use or emotional attachment to the technology rather than impaired control. The findings suggest that LLMs are becoming a habitual component of professional software engineering practice. While most reported use appears functional, the results indicate the importance of promoting appropriate reliance by supporting trust calibration, professional judgment, and verification throughout software development.

</details>


### [188] [JTA: Joint Testability Architecture for Scenario-Based Validation of Safety-Critical Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05594)
*Wenyao Xue,Jiandi Wang,Yichen Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出联合可测试性架构（JTA），将场景、测试系统和被测系统三者作为统一分析对象，通过可控性、可观测性和可隔离性三个维度，以及三个域、三座桥梁和"分析-设计-评估-优化"循环，系统性地建模和评估安全关键软件的验证能力。通过ArduPilot失效保护验证的案例分析展示其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可测试性研究大多以工件为中心，缺乏架构层面的支持来推理场景、测试系统和被测系统的联合验证能力。安全关键软件的验证充分性不仅取决于被测系统本身，还取决于关键场景的受控构建、证据的对齐编排以及异常结果的可归因性，这些问题缺乏统一的架构框架来处理。

Method: 提出联合可测试性架构（JTA）：将场景、测试系统和被测系统视为单一分析设计对象；沿可控性、可观测性、可隔离性三个维度刻画验证能力；通过三个域、三座桥梁和分析-设计-评估-优化循环组织这些维度；引入场景契约、联合能力评估、验证盲区识别和面向桥梁的设计行动，将能力差距映射为控制点、证据组织和归因边界的具体改进。

Result: 通过对ArduPilot失效保护验证的示例分析表明：链路丢失（link-loss）场景相对成熟，而状态估计异常场景更难验证，原因是证据对齐和归因语义较弱。这验证了JTA在识别验证盲区和定位能力短板方面的有效性。

Conclusion: JTA并非替代现有测试或安全分析技术，而是为安全关键软件中基于场景的验证提供架构基础，支撑验证能力的建模、设计和评估。它填补了可测试性研究中缺乏联合架构支持的空白。

Abstract: Validation adequacy in safety-critical software depends on more than the system under test. Critical scenarios must be constructed under controlled conditions, execution evidence must be aligned into verdict-ready form, and abnormal outcomes must be attributable to actionable causes. Existing testability research remains largely artifact-centric and offers little architectural support for reasoning about the combined capability of the scenario, the test system, and the system under test. Joint Testability Architecture (JTA) addresses this gap by treating those three elements as a single object of analysis and design. It characterizes validation capability along three dimensions--controllability, observability, and isolability--and organizes them through three domains, three bridges, and an analysis-design-evaluation-refinement loop. JTA also introduces scenario contracts, joint capability assessment, validation blind-spot identification, and bridge-oriented design actions that map capability gaps to concrete improvements in control points, evidence organization, and attribution boundaries. An illustrative analysis of ArduPilot failsafe validation shows that link-loss scenarios are comparatively mature, whereas state-estimation anomaly scenarios remain harder to validate because evidence alignment and attribution semantics are weaker. JTA is not a replacement for existing testing or safety-analysis techniques; it provides an architectural basis for modeling, designing, and assessing scenario-based validation in safety-critical software.

</details>


### [189] [Towards Competence-Based Management for Open Source Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05599)
*Sabahat Younas,Márcia Moraes,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过收集源码相关代码指标来评估开源软件（OSS）贡献者能力的方法，并构建了一个能力模型来预测解决任务所需的能力水平，旨在识别和培养能够接替核心贡献者的候选人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源项目中核心贡献者流失日益严重，威胁项目生存。然而寻找替代贡献者十分困难，因为缺乏对候选人能力的精确了解，以往的评估缺乏对能力深度的理解，无法准确判断贡献者的真实能力水平。

Method: 通过收集贡献者提交的源代码相关的代码度量指标来评估贡献者的能力，并提出一个能力模型，该模型能够预测解决任务所需的能力水平。

Result: 提供了包含代码、数据和文档的复现包（https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21605122），用于支持研究的可复现性和验证。

Conclusion: 通过正确评估贡献者能力，可以有效识别和培训候选人以接替核心贡献者，从而缓解核心贡献者流失对OSS项目健康的影响。

Abstract: Contributors to Open Source Software (OSS) projects are vital to maintaining the health of both communities and projects. However, the number of projects experiencing core contributors' disengagement has increased to the point that it risks the projects' survival. Finding new contributors to replace the workforce is challenging and time-intensive due to several factors, including a lack of precise knowledge about potential candidates' competences, which may need to be confirmed through interviews and exams. Previous studies provided indications of the contributor's competences, although they lack depth in understanding competence levels, which can result in poor knowledge about the contributor's capabilities. To address this gap, we assess contributors' competence by collecting code metrics related to source code from contributions. We also propose a competence model able to predict the competence level required to solve tasks. By properly assessing contributors' competences, we can identify and train candidates to replace core contributors. Our replication package, including code, data, and documentation, is available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21605122

</details>


### [190] [Keeping Models and Code in Sync: Roundtrip Engineering for Tactical Domain-Driven Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05612)
*Weixing Zhang,Mario Herb,Wai Chung Dorothy Cheng,Michael Wagner,Bowen Jiang,Tianhai Liu,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出JDomInO，一个面向战术DDD的双向同步工具链，通过共享元模型保持Java代码库与领域模型的一致性。前向路径（模型→代码）已在酒店管理场景中全面验证，覆盖全部12种构建块类型；反向路径（代码→模型）的映射逻辑已通过单元测试。该工具生成的领域模型还可作为AI代码助手的精度上下文层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDD为复杂业务逻辑提供共享词汇，但实际开发中模型与代码经常脱节：代码变更速度超过模型更新，或模型修订未同步到代码库。这种漂移使得领域模型失去其作为沟通工具的价值。

Method: JDomInO通过共享元模型连接Java代码库与领域模型，实现双向同步：1) 前向路径：从领域模型确定性生成Java代码结构；2) 反向路径：从既有Java代码重构领域模型。

Result: 前向路径已在酒店管理系统场景中全面验证，覆盖元模型中全部12种构建块类型；反向路径的映射逻辑已通过单元测试，端到端验证正在进行中。此外，论文提出结构化领域模型可为AI代码助手提供精度上下文层，帮助其尊重聚合边界和DDD语义。

Conclusion: JDomInO展示了双向同步工具链在维持模型-代码一致性方面的可行性，并揭示了结构化领域模型作为AI辅助编程上下文的潜在价值，但其反向路径的端到端验证尚未完成，需要进一步工作确认其在实际系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Domain-Driven Design gives teams a shared vocabulary for complex business logic, but that vocabulary only stays useful as long as the model and the code agree with each other. In practice, they drift apart: code changes outpace the model, or model revisions never make it into the codebase. This paper presents JDomInO, a bidirectional synchronization toolchain for tactical DDD that keeps a Java codebase and its domain model connected through a shared metamodel, with the goal of keeping the two in sync as the system evolves. JDomInO generates Java code structure deterministically from a domain model (forward path) and reconstructs a domain model from existing Java code (reverse path). The forward path has been fully validated on a Hotel Management scenario covering all 12 building block types in the metamodel; the reverse path's mapping logic has passed unit testing, with end-to-end validation underway. We also outline how the structured domain model produced by JDomInO could serve as a precision context layer for AI code assistants, helping them respect aggregate boundaries and DDD semantics that raw source code alone does not convey.

</details>


### [191] [Agent-Based Test Assertion Generation via Diverse Perspective Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05822)
*Dong Wang,Qiaoyu Han,Lin Yang,Jianyi Zhou,Guangtai Liang,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AssertMate，一个基于智能体的断言生成框架，通过实际值构造、多视角期望值预测（代码生成、RAG、CoT）和LLM-as-a-Judge协作机制，显著提升了LLM生成单元测试断言的准确性和可靠性，在Defects4J基准上超越了现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的断言生成方法（如ChatAssert）存在准确率不高、过度依赖过采样、单次提示易受模型随机性影响等问题，需要更可靠的自动化断言生成方案。

Method: AssertMate包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过静态分析和类型感知启发式识别断言目标并构造实际值；(2) 采用代码生成、检索增强生成（RAG）和思维链（CoT）三种智能体从多视角预测期望值；(3) 利用LLM-as-a-Judge协作机制挑选最合适的断言。

Result: 在Defects4J基准上的评估显示，AssertMate在编译成功率、通过率和bug检测能力上显著优于最先进技术；与EvoSuite集成进一步验证了其实用性，实现了更优的变异覆盖率和kill数量；消融研究表明三个组件均有显著且互补的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了聚合多样化视角可以增强基于LLM的断言生成效果，展示了多智能体协作在软件测试自动化中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Test assertions are critical elements of unit tests, serving as checkpoints to validate expected behavior and ensure software correctness. Numerous techniques have been proposed to automate assertion generation, with recent progress notably driven by large language models (LLMs). Despite the promise, existing approaches such as ChatAssert suffer from modest accuracy, heavy reliance on oversampling, and vulnerability to model randomness due to one-shot prompting. To address these limitations, we propose AssertMate, a novel agent-based assertion generation framework that enhances the quality and reliability of LLM-generated assertions through three key components: (1) actual value construction that identifies assertion targets via static analysis and type-aware heuristics; (2) multi-perspective expected value prediction using code generation, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning agents; and (3) an LLM-as-a-Judge collaboration mechanism to select the most appropriate assertion. Evaluation on the Defects4J benchmark demonstrates that AssertMate significantly outperforms state-of-the-art techniques in compilation success and pass rates, along with substantially higher bug detection capabilities. Integration with EvoSuite further validates AssertMate's practicality, yielding superior mutation coverage and kill counts. Ablation studies reveal that each of the three components makes a significant and complementary contribution to the overall performance. This work affirms the great potential of aggregating diverse perspectives to enhance the effectiveness of LLM-based assertion generation.

</details>


### [192] [CodeGrep: An RL-Trained Retrieval Agent for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05886)
*Wuya Chen,Yihao yang,Yang Cao,Yue Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeGrep 是一个 14B 参数的检索智能体，通过 GRPO 端到端训练，使用多轮并行 grep、glob 和 read 工具调用，为下游编码智能体提供候选文件。在 SWE-Bench Verified 上，它保持了解决率（27.0% vs 25.8%）的同时，减少了 15% 的轮次和 19% 的 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 编码智能体（如 Claude Code、OpenHands）在仓库探索上浪费大量 token——平均每解决一个 issue 需要大量轮次和 token 用于 grep、glob、view_file 等检索操作，而非实际修补代码。压缩这一检索开销是提升整体效率的关键。

Method: 1) 训练一个 14B 的 CodeGrep 检索智能体，使用 GRPO 端到端强化学习；2) 从 67K 开源智能体轨迹中通过 CATM 挖掘监督信号；3) 构建 Git-worktree 多轮强化学习环境；4) 在 advantage 层而非 reward 层施加效率信号，以减少 KL 漂移并提升下游效率。

Result: 在 500 个 SWE-Bench Verified 实例上，CodeGrep 解决率为 27.0%（基线为 25.8%），在已解决实例上减少 15% 轮次和 19% token。检索器下游效用存在精度阈值：BM25（precision 0.375）有害、Jina（0.445）中性、CodeGrep（0.677）有效降低 roll-out 成本。

Conclusion: 检索精度是决定检索器能否真正提升下游编码智能体效率的关键因素。通过将检索器从编码智能体中分离并进行专用强化学习训练，可以显著降低 token 消耗而不损失解决率。作者将开源模型、训练流程、RL 环境和评测工具。

Abstract: Modern LLM coding agents such as Claude Code and OpenHands share a common inefficiency: they spend much of their token budget finding the file to patch, rather than patching it. On SWE-Bench Verified, a 30B OpenHands agent averages 23 rounds and 631K tokens per resolved issue, with many calls spent on grep, glob, and view_file during repository exploration. We introduce CodeGrep, a 14B retrieval agent trained end-to-end with GRPO to issue multi-turn parallel grep, glob, and read tool calls and return candidate files to a frozen downstream coding agent.
  On all 500 SWE-Bench Verified instances, CodeGrep preserves resolve rate while substantially improving efficiency: 27.0% versus 25.8% for the no-retrieval baseline, with 15% fewer rounds and 19% fewer tokens on resolved instances. Across retrievers, downstream utility follows a precision threshold: BM25 with precision 0.375 degrades the agent, Jina with precision 0.445 is neutral, and CodeGrep with precision 0.677 crosses the threshold at which retrieval begins to reduce rollout cost. To enable this study, we mine supervision from 67K open-source agent trajectories using CATM and build a Git-worktree environment for multi-turn agent RL. In our setting, applying the efficiency signal at the advantage layer rather than the reward layer reduces KL drift and translates cleanly into downstream efficiency. We will release the model, training pipeline, RL environment, and evaluation harnesses.

</details>


### [193] [Mapping the Emerging Curriculum for AI-Assisted Software Engineering via Syllabus Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05898)
*Francis Geng,Anshul Shah,Mia Chen,Paul Denny,Juho Leinonen,Bill Griswold,Gerald Soosai Raj,Leo Porter*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过分析23个公开的、明确涉及生成式AI的软件工程课程大纲，采用迭代定性编码方法，从学习目标、评估方式、主题和AI工具等维度揭示了这一新兴课程领域的共性与差异，为未来课程设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI编码工具正在重塑专业软件开发实践，大学需要设计相关课程来帮助学生适应AI辅助开发工作流程。然而，这一新兴课程领域尚缺乏系统性研究，通过分析现有课程大纲可以获取实证证据，了解该领域如何被定义，并为未来课程设计提供依据。

Method: 收集并筛选了23个公开可获取的、满足特定标准（高年级、计学分、明确涵盖软件工程中的生成式AI）的课程大纲和教学材料，采用迭代定性编码方法对课程的学习目标、评估方式、主题内容及记录的AI工具进行系统分析。

Result: 分析揭示了这些课程在目标、内容和工具使用上的共性与差异，展现了当前AI辅助软件工程课程领域的整体图景。

Conclusion: 该研究为研究者和教育者提供了关于新兴AI软件工程课程领域的实证洞察，为未来此类课程的开发与设计提供了数据支撑和方向指引。

Abstract: As Generative AI coding tools reshape professional software development, universities have begun designing courses to prepare students for AI-assisted development workflows. By analyzing the syllabi of these courses, we can gather empirical evidence about these courses, reveal how this emerging curricular area is being defined, and gain guidance for future curriculum design. We analyzed 23 publicly available syllabi and course materials of upper-division, credit-bearing courses that meet specific criteria, including explicitly addressing Generative AI in software engineering. Through iterative qualitative coding, we characterized courses' learning objectives, assessments, topics, and documented AI tools. Our analysis reveals commonalities and differences among these courses that allow researchers and educators to study and develop future courses.

</details>


### [194] [Escaping the Self-Repair Trap: Improving Test Oracle Generation via Dual-Context Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05917)
*Kefan Li,Hongyue Yu,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DCAware框架，用于LLM回归预言（regression-oracle）补全任务。作者指出当前基于迭代自修复和执行反馈的方法存在"自修复陷阱"（Self-Repair Trap）——模型在迭代中倾向于生成更容易满足但更难发现故障的断言。DCAware采用非迭代、计算高效的设计，通过整合结构化静态上下文和选择性检索的动态状态，在生成健壮预言的同时避免反馈退化。实验证明DCAware在故障发现有效性上优于现有方法，且计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM回归预言生成方法过度依赖迭代自修复和执行反馈，但执行成功（execution success）仅是一个代理目标，与真正目标——生成能有效发现故障的预言——存在错配。这种错配导致"自修复陷阱"：迭代修复使模型不断生成更容易满足却更不敏感的断言，最终损害预言质量。作者旨在打破这一局限，探索非迭代但更有效的替代路径。

Method: DCAware的核心思想是用高质量上下文替换多轮迭代修复。其方法包括两部分：1) 将结构化静态上下文（如代码结构信息）整合进生成过程；2) 选择性检索动态状态（如运行时的变量值信息）作为补充。通过这种高信噪比的上下文接地（contextual grounding），在单轮推理中实现精确且健壮的预言生成，完全绕开迭代反馈循环。

Result: 基于执行测试和变异测试（mutation testing）的广泛实验表明：DCAware在故障发现有效性上持续优于现有方法，同时保持较高的执行成功率，并以显著更低的计算成本实现这些改进。消融研究验证了静态上下文和动态状态检索各自对性能的贡献。

Conclusion: 在回归预言设置下，提升上下文质量比增加迭代修复复杂度更有效。这挑战了当前修复类方法的主流范式，提示研究社区应重新审视"迭代越多越好"的假设，将更多资源投入到上下文工程（context engineering）而非循环修复机制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for regression-oracle completion, where a test prefix is given and the current program version is treated as expected behavior. Recent approaches increasingly rely on iterative self-repair and execution feedback, but optimizing execution success does not necessarily yield strong fault-revealing oracles. This objective, widely adopted in repair-based methods, serves only as a proxy and may be misaligned with the true goal of oracle generation. Such misalignment biases the repair process, giving rise to a feedback-driven degeneration that we term the Self-Repair Trap, where iterative repair progressively drives models toward assertions that are easier to satisfy but less effective at detecting faults. To address this issue, we propose DCAware, a computationally efficient, non-iterative framework that prioritizes high signal-to-noise contextual grounding over multi-round repair. DCAware integrates structured static context with selectively retrieved dynamic states, enabling precise and robust oracle generation without iterative feedback loops. Extensive experiments based on execution and mutation testing show that DCAware consistently improves fault-revealing effectiveness while maintaining high execution success, outperforming prior methods with substantially lower computational cost. Our results suggest that improving contextual quality is more effective than adding iterative repair complexity in the studied regression-oracle setting.

</details>


### [195] [Expertise-Based Developer Assignment for Long-Term Software Components in Open-Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05919)
*Faheem Ullah,Babar Shah,William Shanks,Ayesha Mohsin,Abrar Ullah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Git提交历史建模开发者专业技能的Web应用，用于自动将开发者分配到新项目的长期任务组件中。该系统利用开发者过往的Git提交数据来评估其专长领域，测试表明服务器端数据缓存可将最坏情况速度提升9.86倍，且72.4%的任务目标开发者出现在系统推荐的前10名中（共47名候选人）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有大量工作致力于自动化特定任务的开发者分配，但很少有研究解决新项目中基于专业技能的长期组件（而非一次性任务）的开发者分配问题。将不匹配开发者专长的任务分配出去会导致完成时间变慢和代码可维护性降低。

Method: 该方法构建了一个Web应用，通过分析开发者过往的Git提交记录来建模其专业技能，然后基于这些信息自动将开发者分配到新项目中的最优任务。系统采用了服务器端数据缓存来优化性能，并对比了不同后端模型的运行速度。

Result: 测试结果表明系统运行速度取决于后端模型的选择，但服务器端数据缓存将最坏情况下的速度提升了9.86倍。在推荐准确性方面，72.4%的任务其目标开发者出现在系统推荐的前10名中（总共47名候选开发者）。

Conclusion: 该Web应用通过Git提交历史有效地建模开发者专业技能，并能在新项目中进行合理的任务分配推荐，且通过缓存优化了系统性能，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Open-source software development through GitHub has enabled countless software projects to be developed by developers from across the world. Assigning the right task to the right developer enables teams to work efficiently. Assigning a task which is not representative of a developer's expertise results in slower completion time and less maintainable code. Whilst much work has been done to automate this assignment for specific jobs, there is little work addressing the assignment of developers to expertise-specific long-term components of a new project. This paper produces a web application that models developers' expertise based on their previous Git commits and automatically assigns them to an optimal task within a new project. Testing showed that the system's speed varies depending on the back-end model used but server-side data caching improved worst-case speeds by a factor of 9.86. 72.4% of tasks had their target developer listed in the top 10, out of a possible 47.

</details>


### [196] [Sensor-Level Fault Diagnosis for Automotive Software Validation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05921)
*Mohammad Abboush,Hamza Ouarrad,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段框架，利用开源指令微调的大语言模型（LLM），在HIL平台上对汽车软件传感器录数据进行故障检测与诊断。首先通过自动化需求检查筛选违规记录，再对滑动窗口信号降维为统计、关系与上下文描述符，嵌入固定提示模板，由4-bit低秩适配（LoRA）的LLM映射到故障位置并附带书面解释。在汽油发动机案例上对四种模型（20亿至80亿参数）进行六类故障注入测试，最小模型达到与最大模型相同的81.6%准确率，且整个适配与评估循环可在单块商用加速器上完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIL平台上汽车软件的预系列验证产生大量多变量传感器记录，人工评审无法满足规模化需求。传统的基于阈值的工具只能报告偏差但无法识别性质与来源；数据驱动的分类器虽然准确但依赖大规模标注数据且决策不透明，难以满足ISO 26262对可追溯性的要求。因此需要一种数据高效且可解释的故障检测与诊断方案。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段在dSPACE实时平台上执行自动化需求检查，仅隔离违反安全需求的记录；第二阶段对违规记录使用滑动窗口提取信号的统计、关系和上下文描述符，嵌入固定提示模板，由4-bit LoRA适配的LLM映射到故障位置，并生成书面论证。测试了四个模型家族（20亿到80亿参数），在汽油发动机案例中注入六类故障。

Result: 最小的模型（20亿参数）在适配后达到了与最大模型（80亿参数）相同的81.6%准确率，而一个参数规模相当的对比模型未能收敛。这表明在任务特定适配下，诊断能力取决于收敛性而非参数数量。整个适配与评估循环可在一张商用加速卡上完成。

Conclusion: 开源指令微调LLM可以作为数据高效且可解释的故障检测与诊断引擎嵌入验证循环。所提出的两阶段框架在保障ISO 26262可追溯性的同时，实现了高精度诊断，并且模型收敛性比参数规模更重要这一发现为资源受限场景下LLM在汽车软件验证中的应用提供了实践指导。

Abstract: The pre-series validation of automotive software on hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) platforms produces large volumes of multivariate sensor recordings whose assessment against functional safety requirements exceeds what manual review can sustain at campaign scale. Threshold-based tooling reports that a deviation has occurred but neither identifies its nature nor locates its source, while data-driven classifiers, although accurate, rely on large labelled datasets and return opaque decisions that sit uneasily with the traceability demanded by ISO 26262. This study examines whether open-source instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs), given a textual description of sensor behaviour, can serve as data-efficient and interpretable engines for fault detection and diagnosis inside the validation loop. A two-phase framework is proposed: automated requirement checking on a dSPACE real-time platform first isolates the recordings that violate a safety requirement, and only these are inspected, with sliding windows of the signals reduced to statistical, relational, and contextual descriptors, embedded in a fixed prompt, and mapped by a 4-bit low-rank-adapted LLM to a fault location accompanied by a written justification. Four model families ranging from two to eight billion parameters were adapted and tested on a gasoline-engine case study spanning six injected fault classes. The smallest model matched the largest at 81.6\% accuracy, whereas a comparably sized model failed to converge, indicating that diagnostic competence under task-specific adaptation follows convergence rather than parameter count, with the entire adapt-and-evaluate cycle fitting on a single commodity accelerator.

</details>


### [197] [AgentExecutor: Partial Code Execution via Agentic Context Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05959)
*Junkai Chen,Chengran Yang,Xing Hu,Zhenhao Li,Xin Xia,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentExecutor，一个基于多智能体框架的部分代码执行方法，通过三阶段设计（执行环境准备、动态探索与迭代优化、前缀演化）解决任意代码片段难以执行的问题，在两个数据集上达到94%和90%的代码覆盖率，优于现有最优方法Treefix，同时显著降低执行时间和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态程序分析需要执行代码片段，但任意代码片段的执行因缺少上下文和不完整依赖而面临挑战。现有方法（如LExecutor和Treefix）利用语言模型推断缺失信息，但受限于有限的动作空间和反馈机制，以及僵化的优化策略，导致效率和效果受限。

Method: AgentExecutor采用多智能体框架，包含三个核心阶段：(1) 执行环境准备；(2) 基于迭代优化的动态探索，利用LLM智能体的思考-行动-反馈循环，支持更丰富的动作空间（如创建资源文件、解析环境配置）；(3) 通过程序合成进行前缀演化。同时采用自适应优化策略，包括覆盖引导的上下文剪枝和前缀演化。

Result: 在两个广泛使用的数据集（Stack Overflow代码片段和开源项目代码）上，AgentExecutor分别达到94%和90%的代码覆盖率，相比最先进方法Treefix提升19.9%和13.8%。执行时间最多减少80.3%，成本最多降低56.6%。

Conclusion: AgentExecutor通过多智能体协作和自适应优化策略，为部分代码执行提供了既有效又高效的解决方案，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Executing code snippets is essential for dynamic program analysis, but it remains challenging to execute an arbitrary code snippet due to issues like missing context and incomplete dependencies. Existing approaches to partial code execution, such as LExecutor and Treefix, leverage the power of language models to infer missing information and enable execution. However, they suffer from (i) limited action spaces and feedback, and (ii) rigid optimization strategies, which restrict their effectiveness and efficiency. In this paper, we propose AgentExecutor, a novel multi-agent framework for partial code execution. Our approach introduces a three-phase design: execution environment preparation, dynamic exploration with iterative refinement, and prefix evolution via program synthesis. Supported by the power of LLM agents who can think, act, and get feedback iteratively, AgentExecutor is able to autonomously explore a richer action space, enabling diverse operations such as creating resource files and resolving environment configuration. Furthermore, it adopts adaptive optimization strategies, including coverage-guided context pruning and prefix evolution via program synthesis, to systematically improve the execution quality of partial code. We evaluate AgentExecutor on two widely used datasets comprising Stack Overflow snippets and open-source project code. The results show that AgentExecutor achieves up to 94% and 90% code coverage, outperforming the state-of-the-art approach Treefix by 19.9% and 13.8%, respectively. In addition, AgentExecutor significantly reduces execution time (by up to 80.3%) and cost (by up to 56.6%). These findings demonstrate that AgentExecutor provides an effective and efficient solution for partial code execution.

</details>


### [198] [LangChoiceBench: Measuring and Explaining Programming-Language Choice in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06041)
*Lukas Twist,Twm Stone,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LangChoiceBench基准，用于系统测量LLM在项目级代码生成中的Python偏好。通过评估25个LLM在28个Python并非最优选择的项目上的表现，发现Python被过度选择、推荐与实现一致性低，并识别出"幻觉证据"和推理与代码矛盾等失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在项目级代码生成中表现出强烈的Python偏好，但目前缺乏系统化的方法来衡量不同新模型在这一行为上的表现，需要建立统一评估基准。

Method: 构建LangChoiceBench基准，覆盖7个软件领域、28个Python通常不是最佳默认选择的项目；评估25个多样化LLM，并分析9,826条推理轨迹。

Result: 发现Python仍被过度选择，推荐-实现一致性低，小型开放权重模型表现更强的Python偏好和更低的语言多样性；大多数Python选择是自动化的或出于便利性，而非基于项目需求考量；存在"幻觉证据"和推理与代码自相矛盾等失败模式。

Conclusion: LLM的Python偏好问题普遍且系统化，需要新的方法缓解这一偏差，提高多语言代码生成的多样性和一致性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been shown to exhibit strong Python preferences when generating project-level code, but there is currently no systematic way to measure this behaviour across new models. To bridge this gap, we introduce LangChoiceBench, a project-level code-generation benchmark for measuring Python preference, recommendation-implementation consistency, and language diversity. LangChoiceBench covers 28 projects across seven software areas where Python is often a poor default. We evaluate 25 diverse LLMs and find that Python remains heavily over-selected, recommendation-implementation consistency is low, and smaller open-weight models generally show stronger Python preference and lower language diversity. We further analyse 9,826 reasoning traces and find that most Python choices are automatic or driven primarily by ease, rather than explicit consideration of project requirements. In a smaller but important set of cases, models fabricate contextual support for choosing Python - a failure mode we call phantom evidence - or produce code that contradicts the language selected in their own reasoning.

</details>


### [199] [In Terms of Explainability: Refining Requirements for Self-Explainable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06049)
*Arno Leue,Akhila Bairy,Maike Schwammberger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对自主和软件密集型系统中可解释性缺乏统一定义和标准的问题，提出了统一的解释性定义、结构化的解释性需求，并将解释质量（goodness）纳入定义中，以支持正式标准的制定和系统认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主和软件密集型系统日益复杂且承担更多责任，透明性和可解释性成为关键研究焦点。然而目前缺乏普遍适用的可解释性定义和开发指南，且欧盟AI法案和IEEE透明度标准7001-2021已明确承认对可解释性标准的需求。

Method: 通过分析和组合现有解释性定义，提出统一定义；构建结构化的解释性需求分类体系（taxonomy）；将解释质量（explanation goodness）及解释正确性纳入统一定义中。

Result: 提出了统一的解释性定义、结构化的解释性需求分类体系，并将解释质量整合进定义框架，为可解释系统的正式标准制定奠定基础。

Conclusion: 该统一的分类体系有助于推动可解释系统的形式化标准建立，也是迈向可解释系统认证或审计的重要一步。

Abstract: Autonomous and software-intensive systems have been growing in occurrence, complexity, and assumed responsibility. Due to the high complexity of these systems, properties like transparency and explainability must be a focus of investigation. To date, no universally applicable definition and guide for the development of (self-)explainable systems exists. A need for explainability standards has already been recognized in the EU AI Act and the IEEE Transparency Standard 7001-2021. To address this need, we propose unified definitions in explainability based on an analysis and combination of existing definitions. Additionally, we present structured explainability requirements that are necessary to build (self-)explainable systems. By analysing the resulting taxonomy, we propose the incorporation of explanation goodness and thus correctness of explanations into the unified definitions. With our approach, we support the development of formal standards for (self-)explainable systems. Establishing such a uniform taxonomy also is a beneficial step towards certifying or auditing explainable systems.

</details>


### [200] [DCAS: Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding to Internalize Planning across Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06113)
*Kishanthan Thangarajah,Boyuan Chen,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文识别了CLI软件工程智能体领域的一个关键问题：开源模型微调数据几乎全部来自OpenHands脚手架，导致模型在训练脚手架下表现良好，但在其他脚手架下性能严重退化。作者提出规划结构是导致这一退化的核心因素（区分显式规划与隐式规划），并引入DCAS（解耦CLI智能体脚手架）——一个后端替换拦截层，可在不修改脚手架的情况下路由API流量，支持跨脚手架评估与规划感知轨迹收集。实验证明规划质量是高杠杆组件，且基于DCAS收集的少量规划感知轨迹微调可跨脚手架一致提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源生态中基于CLI的软件工程智能体训练环境高度同质化，几乎所有微调数据都来自OpenHands单一脚手架。这导致模型在训练脚手架下得分高，但在其他脚手架下显著退化；而未经微调的基座模型没有这种分歧，说明性能差距是微调引入的且与训练脚手架的约定绑定。作者旨在找出这种脚手架特定行为，并提出解决方案以弥合跨脚手架性能差距。

Method: 作者提出两个核心概念：（1）显式规划——执行前产生的第一类人工制品；（2）隐式规划——塑造智能体循环执行的结构约定。基于此假设，本文引入DCAS（解耦CLI智能体脚手架），这是一个后端替换拦截层，能在不修改脚手架的情况下在任意CLI脚手架与后端模型间路由API流量，实现跨脚手架评估和规划感知轨迹收集。通过受控的规划源干预实验验证规划质量是高杠杆组件。

Result: 受控的规划源干预实验证实规划质量是高杠杆组件，其收益超过观察到的跨脚手架性能下降。在单一脚手架下使用少量DCAS收集的规划感知轨迹微调的模型，在非训练脚手架上获得一致的性能提升。此外，显式规划与隐式规划两种规划感知在训练数据中可被经验性地分离。

Conclusion: 弥合脚手架间性能差距需要将规划从固定的脚手架人工制品转变为可学习的模型能力。DCAS提供了实现这一转变的基础设施，使得模型能够在跨脚手架环境下学习鲁棒的规划能力，而不是过度适应单一训练脚手架的约定。

Abstract: CLI-based software-engineering agents have matured rapidly, yet the open ecosystem has converged on a single training environment: trajectory datasets used to fine-tune open models are collected almost exclusively under OpenHands. Models fine-tuned on this data score well under OpenHands but degrade substantially when deployed under any non-training scaffold. Untrained base models do not show this divergence, indicating the gap is fine-tuning-induced and tied to the conventions of the training scaffold. We argue that a load-bearing scaffold-specific behavior is planning structure, in two senses this paper distinguishes: explicit planning, a pre-execution plan produced as a first-class artifact, and implicit planning, the structural conventions that shape execution throughout the agent loop. Under this hypothesis, closing the gap requires moving planning from a fixed scaffold artifact to a learned model capability. We introduce Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding (DCAS), a backend-substitution interception layer that routes API traffic between any CLI scaffold and any backend model without modifying the scaffold, enabling cross-scaffold evaluation and planning-aware trajectory collection. Using DCAS, a controlled plan-source intervention confirms planning quality is a high-leverage component, with gains exceeding the cross-scaffold drops we observe. A model fine-tuned on a small set of DCAS-collected planning-aware trajectories under a single scaffold gains consistently across non-training scaffolds, and the two senses of planning are empirically separable in training data.

</details>


### [201] [Learning Globally Reusable Skills for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06153)
*Chen Yang,Jiashuo Tian,Ziqi Wang,Xinyin Liu,Meiru Ye,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GSE（全局化技能进化框架），通过技能关系图（SRG）建模技能间关联，并采用基于聚类的技能整合与重放驱动的验证机制，解决现有LLM智能体技能进化中局部更新导致的过拟合和泛化不足问题，在软件工程任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的技能进化方法将技能更新视为一系列局部更新，忽略了技能之间的关系，导致产生过拟合的技能更新，无法在跨任务场景中良好泛化。这限制了LLM智能体持续学习和改进的能力。

Method: GSE框架包含三个核心组件：1) 技能关系图（SRG）：显式建模并协同进化技能间的关系，保持技能库的一致性；2) 基于聚类的技能整合：从局部更新中抽象出可复用的通用能力，提升泛化性；3) 重放驱动的验证：通过重放机制防止过拟合和行为退化。

Result: 在bug触发测试生成和误报缺陷报告过滤两个软件工程任务上，GSE在OpenHands和mini-SWE-agent两个编码智能体上均取得最佳精确率、召回率和F1分数。测试生成任务提升精确率6.1%~34.1%、召回率31.8%~180.0%；误报过滤任务提升精确率15.4%~96.4%、召回率13.1%~19.8%。在内部工业智能体上部署后F1分数提升61.4%。

Conclusion: GSE通过全局化的技能进化视角，有效解决了现有方法中技能关系缺失和泛化不足的问题，在多个智能体平台和任务上均展现了显著的性能提升，并验证了其在工业场景中的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Automated skill evolution enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to continuously improve without expensive retraining. However, existing approaches typically treat skill evolution as a sequence of local updates, overlooking relationships among skills and often producing overfitted skill updates that fail to generalize across tasks. We propose GSE, a globalized skill evolution framework that jointly optimizes skill compatibility and skill generalization. To preserve consistency across the skill bank, GSE maintains a Skill Relation Graph (SRG) that explicitly models and co-evolves inter-skill relationships. To improve generalization, GSE performs cluster-based skill consolidation to abstract reusable capabilities from local updates and employs replay-driven verification to prevent overfitting and behavioral regressions. We evaluate GSE on two representative software engineering tasks: bug-revealing test generation and false-positive bug report filtering. Across two state-of-the-art coding agents, OpenHands and mini-SWE-agent, GSE consistently achieves the best precision, recall, and F1-score. Compared with existing evolution techniques, GSE improves precision and recall by 6.1%~34.1% and 31.8%~180.0% for test generation, and by 15.4%~96.4% and 13.1%~19.8% for false-positive filtering. Deployment on an internal industrial agent further yields a 61.4% improvement in F1-score, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalizability of GSE for evolving effective skills.

</details>


### [202] [Predicting Agile Success: The Critical Few Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06228)
*Ridewaan Hanslo,Maureen Tanner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究采用实证主义方法，通过PLS-SEM分析识别敏捷软件开发项目的关键成功因素（CSF），并开发了一个可用于评估和衡量项目成功的新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管敏捷项目的成功率高于传统软件项目管理方法，但整体成功率仍然较低，大量项目仍被视为"受挑战"或"失败"。这种低成功率归因于项目没有被严格地根据敏捷项目的关键成功因素（CSF）和当代项目成功标准进行评估。

Method: 采用实证主义（positivist）研究方法来调查敏捷软件开发项目成功的关键成功因素，通过敏捷从业者的视角收集数据，使用PLS-SEM（偏最小二乘结构方程模型）配合SmartPLS软件进行数据分析，检验各构念与项目成功标准之间的显著关系假设。

Result: 研究发现少数关键因素对敏捷项目成功有显著贡献，并开发了一个新颖的模型，可用于评估和衡量项目成功。

Conclusion: 该研究提出并验证了一个新的模型，该模型可用于敏捷软件开发项目的成功评估，有助于解决敏捷项目成功率较低的问题。

Abstract: While Agile projects are more successful than traditional software development project management approaches, their overall success rate remains relatively low, with a high percentage of projects still deemed challenged or failed. This low project success rate is attributed to projects not being rigorously evaluated against critical success factors (CSF) of Agile projects and contemporary project success criteria. To identify the CSF that contribute to Agile software development project success as perceived by Agile practitioners, this study used a positivist approach to investigate the CSF of Agile software development projects. PLS-SEM with SmartPLS was used to analyse the data and test the hypotheses to identify significant relationships between the constructs and project success criteria. This research found that a few critical factors significantly contribute to Agile project success. The study developed a novel model that can be used to evaluate and measure project success.

</details>


### [203] [Automatic Translation of Unstructured Requirements into Linear Temporal Logic through Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06287)
*Alexandra Newcomb,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种当代通用大语言模型（LLMs）在无需任务特定微调的情况下，使用少样本提示策略将非结构化自然语言需求直接转换为线性时序逻辑（LTL）公式的能力。在包含15个结构多样化需求的基准上进行了450个候选公式的实验，通过人工语义评估、pass@k和自一致性指标进行性能评估，结果表明现代LLM可以作为半自动化形式化工作流的前端助手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非结构化自然语言需求自动转化为形式化规范是需求工程和形式化方法领域的长期挑战，特别是对于依赖精确数学规范来进行验证的安全关键和任务关键系统。作者希望探究当代现成的LLM是否能帮助弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 研究采用少样本提示策略，对六种现代LLM在15个结构各异的异构需求基准上进行测试。每个需求-模型组合生成5次独立样本，共获得450个候选LTL公式。评估通过三种方式：人工语义评估、pass@k（k=1,3,5）指标、以及衡量跨随机试验语法可重现性的自一致性度量。此外还结合模型生成的解释和基于时间线的LTL可视化来考虑非专家的可理解性。

Result: 结果表明，当代通用LLM在非结构化自然语言到LTL的转换任务上能够实现实际显著的性能，且无需任务特定的微调。研究还提供了人工语义评估、pass@k和自一致性等多维度的量化评估结果。

Conclusion: 现代LLM正逐渐成为半自动化形式化工作流中可行的前端助手。将LLM生成的公式与自然语言解释及时间线LTL可视化相结合，可以增强非专家对形式化规范的理解，为安全关键系统的形式化验证流程提供辅助。

Abstract: Automatically translating unstructured natural language requirements into formal specifications remains a challenge in requirements engineering and formal methods, particularly for safety- and mission-critical systems whose verification depends on mathematically precise specifications. This paper evaluates whether contemporary off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can help bridge this gap by generating Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas directly from unstructured requirements. The study examines six modern LLMs using a few-shot prompting strategy on a heterogeneous benchmark of 15 structurally varied requirements. Five independent generations were collected for each requirement-model pair, yielding 450 candidate LTL formulas in total. Performance was assessed through manual semantic evaluation, pass@k for k in {1, 3, 5}, and a self-consistency measure capturing syntactic reproducibility across stochastic trials. The results indicate that current general-purpose LLMs can achieve practically significant performance on the unstructured NL-to-LTL task without task-specific fine-tuning. The study also considers understandability for non-experts by pairing generated formulas with model-produced natural language explanations and discussing the complementary use of timeline-based LTL visualization. The findings suggest that modern LLMs are becoming viable front-end assistants for semi-automated formalization workflows.

</details>


### [204] [A Study of LLMs' Preferences for Libraries and Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17181)
*Lukas Twist,Mark Harman,Don Syme,Joost Noppen,Helen Yannakoudakis,Detlef Nauck,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM在代码生成中对库和编程语言的选择偏好进行了实证研究，覆盖8种LLM，发现它们过度使用NumPy等流行库（最多45%为非必需使用），并强烈偏好Python（即使在不适合的场景下仍占58%），表明模型优先考虑熟悉度和流行度而非任务适配性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成评估仅关注功能正确性或语法有效性，忽视了模型在库选择和编程语言选择等关键设计决策上的能力，这一空白需要被填补。

Method: 对8种多样的LLM进行实证研究，分析它们在代码生成中对库和编程语言的选择偏好，包括在需要高性能项目初始化的任务中观察语言选择倾向。

Result: 发现LLM强烈倾向过度使用NumPy等广泛采用的库（最高45%的情况下非必需）；强烈偏好Python作为默认语言，即使Python并非最优选择时仍占58%的主导地位，而Rust从未被使用。

Conclusion: LLM在代码生成中优先考虑熟悉度和流行度而非任务适配性和最优性，需要进行针对性微调、数据多样化和引入明确衡量语言与库选择保真度的评估基准。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of large language models (LLMs) in code generation, existing evaluations focus on functional correctness or syntactic validity, overlooking how LLMs make critical design choices such as which library or programming language to use. To fill this gap, we perform the first empirical study of LLMs' preferences for libraries and programming languages when generating code, covering eight diverse LLMs. We observe a strong tendency to overuse widely adopted libraries such as NumPy; in up to 45% of cases, this usage is not required and deviates from the ground-truth solutions. The LLMs we study also show a significant preference toward Python as their default language. For high-performance project initialisation tasks where Python is not the optimal language, it remains the dominant choice in 58% of cases, and Rust is not used once. These results highlight how LLMs prioritise familiarity and popularity over suitability and task-specific optimality; underscoring the need for targeted fine-tuning, data diversification, and evaluation benchmarks that explicitly measure language and library selection fidelity.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Nesting，一个多智能体协作框架，将现有企业应用封装为自主AI智能体，以分层嵌套结构组织，通过中心协调器进行任务分解和动态调度，并暴露统一对话界面，实现跨应用查询和流程编排，解决企业应用集成中数据孤岛和流程碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业运营依赖多个异构业务系统和信息应用，导致严重的数据孤岛和流程碎片化。传统集成方式（ESB、API网关、RPA）存在高架构耦合、运维成本攀升和智能化能力有限等固有缺陷，亟需新的集成范式。

Method: 提出Agentic Nesting多智能体协作框架：1) 将现有企业应用封装为自主AI智能体，组织成分层嵌套的"守护拓扑"（stewardship topologies），模拟企业生态系统的组合复杂度；2) 从每个遗留应用中提取数字代理（digital agent proxy），支持自然语言交互和自主操作；3) 通过中心编排器协调多个智能体进行任务分解和动态调度；4) 提供统一对话界面，实现跨应用查询和流程编排。

Result: 论文的主要贡献包括：提出"Application-as-Agent"（应用即智能体）集成范式和"Conversation-as-Integration"（对话即集成）交互哲学，并探索该方法论在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用场景中的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting为企业应用集成提供了一种基于多智能体协作的新范式，通过分层嵌套的智能体拓扑和自然语言驱动的统一交互界面，有望克服传统中间件方案的局限性，实现更灵活、更智能的企业系统编排。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [206] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了点火指数（Ignition Index, I），一种将全局工作空间理论（GWT）的全或无点火预测操作化到Transformer语言模型的标量指标。该指标通过四参数sigmoid拟合每层线性探针准确率与输入信号强度的关系，提取陡峭参数β̂。在11个模型、5个架构家族中验证了9.6倍的选择性，并发现前馈Transformer、递归架构和训练动态中的点火模式差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局工作空间理论（GWT）预测大脑中信息处理存在"全或无"的点火式转变，但这一动态预测从未在Transformer语言模型中得到定量验证。现有机制可解释性研究缺乏将GWT的理论预测与模型内部信息处理联系起来的验证性指标。

Method: 提出Ignition Index指标：对每层线性探针准确率随输入信号强度变化拟合四参数sigmoid曲线，提取陡峭参数β̂作为点火指数。高β̂表示突然的点火式转变，低β̂表示渐进累积。在11个模型（涵盖5个架构家族）上进行验证，使用洗牌标签对照评估选择性。

Result: (1) 前馈Transformer在聚合β̂上超过SSM 89%（p<1e-13, Cohen's d=0.52），Mamba呈现近线性轮廓（缺乏全局广播）；(2) Huginn-3.5B沿迭代轴的点火比深度轴高2.12倍；(3) Pythia-410M在训练第256步检测到相位转变（+67%），先于induction head形成；(4) 点火与模型规模和信号强度的假设未获证实。

Conclusion: Ignition Index提供了GWT动态预测与机制可解释性之间的首个验证性定量桥梁，具有9.6倍测量选择性，并展现出此前文献中未表征的架构级区分能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [207] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Woodpecker Distillation框架，通过利用弱模型生成的对比性局部补丁（成功的与失败的）来构建纠正性教师分布，并将此信号蒸馏到强模型中，从而修复强模型在推理任务中的局部推理错误，提升数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理任务中经常失败，但作者认为这些失败源于中间步骤的局部推理错误，而非整体能力不足。这些错误通常是可修复的——插入弱模型生成的短补丁即可引导强模型走向正确解。然而，直接微调这些补丁或修复轨迹并不能有效内化修正效果，因为有用的信号不在于干预文本本身，而在于它如何重塑模型未来的推理分布。因此需要新的训练框架来提取这一信号。
